## Narration 1200

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-abudawud%3A1204)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 4</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا مُسَدَّدٌ، حَدَّثَنَا أَبُو مُعَاوِيَةَ، عَنِ الْمِسْحَاجِ بْنِ مُوسَى، قَالَ قُلْتُ لأَنَسِ بْنِ مَالِكٍ حَدِّثْنَا مَا، سَمِعْتَ مِنْ، رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏.‏ قَالَ كُنَّا إِذَا كُنَّا مَعَ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم فِي السَّفَرِ فَقُلْنَا زَالَتِ الشَّمْسُ أَوْ لَمْ تَزُلْ صَلَّى الظُّهْرَ ثُمَّ ارْتَحَلَ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1204]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1204" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sunan Abi Dawud · 1204</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sunan Abi Dawud   1204 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Narrated Mishaj b. Musa:I asked Anas b. Malik: Narrate to us what you heard the Messenger of Allah (ﷺ) say. He said: When we travelled along with the Messenger of Allah (ﷺ), we would say: Did the sun pass the meridian or not? But he (the Prophet) would offer the noon prayer and then proceed</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1204]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1204" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sunan Abi Dawud · 1204</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sunan Abi Dawud   1204 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">मिस-हज बिन मूसा कहते हैं कि मैंने अनस बिन मालिक (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से कहा: हम से आप कोई ऐसी हदीथ बयां कीजिये जिसे आप ने रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) से सुना हो (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) के साथ होते तो हम कहते: सूरज ढल गया या नहीं तो आप ज़ोहर पढते फिर कुछ (सफर) करते.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1204]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1204" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">مسحاج بن موسیٰ کہتے ہیں کہ میں نے انس بن مالک رضی اللہ عنہ سے کہا: ہم سے آپ کوئی ایسی حدیث بیان کیجئے جسے آپ نے رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم سے سنا ہو، انہوں نے کہا: جب ہم لوگ سفر میں رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کے ساتھ ہوتے تو ہم کہتے: سورج ڈھل گیا یا نہیں ۱؎ تو آپ ظہر پڑھتے پھر کوچ کرتے۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1204]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1204" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Mis-haj bin Musa kehte hain ke maine Anas bin Malik (Radi Allahu Anhu) se kaha: Hum se aap koi aisi hadith bayan kijiye jise aap ne Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) se suna ho, unhone kaha: Jab hum log safar mein Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ke sath hote to hum kehte: Suraj dhal gaya ya nahi to aap Zohar parhte phir kuch (safar) karte.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1204]</p>
